\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 3. Revision Scope and Non-Scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This revision covers Voice / Singing baseline wording, evidence requirements, claim-scope rules, safety/fairness guardrails, style/subtype boundaries, generic-feedback suppression, material-policy handling and report-behaviour expectations inside the current six-field TapeCoach architecture.\
This revision does 
\f0\b not
\f1\b0  cover backend implementation, schema changes, score-field changes, weighting changes, cap/blocker/verdict changes, role-fit bound changes, server-side recomputation changes, frontend label implementation, comparison-page rendering, PDF/export behaviour, live-output QA or product rollout.\
Out of scope because of production guardrails: adding a musical-interpretation score field, renaming operational score fields, adding a vocal-health score, adding formal subtype rubrics that require schema changes, importing external exam marks, and changing the MT acting + song flow.\
Pending because no live Song / Voice outputs were supplied: live report quality, rendered category labels, comparison-page parity, timestamp rendering parity, score stability and production-output non-regression.\
Preserve without change: six score fields, Song and MT weights, Step 1 / Step 2 pipeline, locked-field enforcement, deterministic scrubs, maximum 3 strengths, maximum 3 improvements, maximum 8 timestamped notes, material-policy guardrails, safety/accessibility scrubs, server-side recomputation and MT acting + song stability.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 4. Audit Recommendation Implementation Matrix\

\f1\b0 \cf0 VOICE-REV-S01 \'97 Purpose and baseline fit\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Voice / Singing assesses whether a song self-tape is submission-ready for the performer\'92s level, audition type and brief context. It improves sung-vocal and song-interpretation specificity inside the existing TapeCoach structure.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Locked Step 1 evidence, performer level, audition type, brief/material context and timestamped observations where available.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 New score fields, new weights, invented observations, Step 2 score/evidence alteration.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R20, R21\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F01, F17\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Baseline controls, report-polish / quality snippets\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves live production architecture.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S02 \'97 Field semantics and category meanings\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In Song and Musical Theatre, 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  means sung-vocal evidence. In Song, 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  means lyric/storytelling/communication through song, not acting-scene performance. Audio and technical are assessability conditions.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Observable sung sound, lyric delivery, communication, audio/video readability and brief/task context.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Singing labels in Acting/Dance-only contexts; acting-scene language in song-only outputs.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R01, R20\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F01\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S001\'96S052, cross-branch context\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Keeps field names and weights unchanged.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S03 \'97 Song / Voice evidence standard\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 A compliant Song report must assess vocal technique and interpretive/song-communication evidence where assessable. Technique-only praise is insufficient.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 At least one observable vocal evidence anchor and one lyric/story/communication anchor when material and audio allow.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Reports that only say \'93good voice\'94, \'93secure pitch\'94 or \'93strong vocal control\'94 without phrase, lyric, timestamp or behaviour evidence.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R02\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F02\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S001\'96S031, S035\'96S040\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves Song vocal-centred weighting.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S04 \'97 Vocal technique evidence\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Vocal technique language must use observable descriptors such as pitch/intonation, rhythm/time, tone/tonal quality, diction/intelligibility, phrasing/shape, dynamic control, range exposure and breath/phrase management where supported.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Specific moment, phrase, timestamp, register/line demand or audible change.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Generic \'93technically excellent\'94, diagnostic vocal-health wording, unsupported method claims.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R03, R17\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F03, F16\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S021\'96S031\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Uses descriptor language only; no grades or marks.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S05 \'97 Lyric storytelling / acting-through-song evidence\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Acting/storytelling through song must be song-framed: lyric intention, addressee, objective, phrase action, emotional arc, character communication or relationship to the song\'92s situation.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Observable lyric/phrase/beat/transition evidence.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Reader, scene-partner, eyeline-to-reader or spoken-scene feedback where no spoken acting exists.\cell

\cf0 R02, R04\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F02, F04\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S002, S009, S010, S017, S021, S023, S026, S027, S035, S036, S040\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Protects song-only outputs from acting-scene leakage.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S06 \'97 Musical interpretation inside existing fields\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Musical interpretation should appear through vocal and acting/storytelling wording: phrasing, shape, dynamics, style, musical communication and lyric meaning. It is not a separate score.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Observable phrasing, dynamic choice, tempo/rhythm relationship, lyric emphasis or stylistic communication.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 A new musical-interpretation score field; vague \'93great musicality\'94 without evidence.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R05\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F02, F03\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S021\'96S031, S035\'96S041\cell

\cf0 Preserves six-field model.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S07 \'97 Style / subtype handling\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Name style/subtype only when brief, material, source context, slate or observable performance supports it. Stronger supported contexts include classical/legit, contemporary MT, classical art song/opera-adjacent, unaccompanied singing, accompanied/backing-track song, audition cuts and MT song in multi-component tapes.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Brief/material evidence, known repertoire context or observable style markers.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Universal style rules; unsupported belt/mix/registration labels; school-specific era rules as global standards.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R06, R07\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F05, F06\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S001\'96S020, S021\'96S031, S035\'96S041\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Prevents subtype overclaim.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S08 \'97 Accompaniment / backing track / self-accompaniment handling\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Treat accompaniment mode as task/context evidence. Balance, audibility and acceptability matter for assessability; access to a better track, pianist or studio must not be scored as vocal merit.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Task requirement, brief instruction, audible balance, visible self-accompaniment or source-supported context.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Universal \'93must use backing track/live pianist\'94 rules; rewarding paid accompanist or professional track access.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R10\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F09, F13\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S001\'96S020, S026, S027, S039\'96S041, S051\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves fairness and material-policy context.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S09 \'97 Self-tape assessability, audio and technical boundaries\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Audio and technical notes should explain whether singing and storytelling are assessable: clear audio, balanced accompaniment, workable acoustics, stable framing and readable capture. These are not vocal merit.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Audio clarity, track/voice balance, room noise/reverb, framing, visibility and recording continuity where observed.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 \'93Clean audio\'94 as vocal quality; weak audio as weak singing unless vocal evidence is fairly assessable.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R08\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F07, F08\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S003, S005, S008, S011, S013, S023, S024, S027, S032\'96S041\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves audio/technical categories.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S10 \'97 Professional presentation / anti-polish boundary\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Professional presentation in Song / Voice is about submission readability and process, not polish, class-coded setup, expensive kit, paid support, charm, appearance or studio quality. Omit presentation notes if no material value.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Locked evidence that presentation affects readability, compliance or assessability.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 \'93Professional self-tape\'94 as praise for expensive production; home setup/resource limits as deficit.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R09\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F13\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S033, S040, S041, S047\'96S051, baseline scrubs\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves presentation as optional and non-personal.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S11 \'97 Material, repertoire and fixed-brief handling\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 If material is fixed, improve the submitted song only. If material is choice-led, repertoire comments must be brief-aware, source-bounded and evidence-led. Avoid universal rules from school/open-call contexts.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Brief/material instruction, supplied song title, audition context, source-supported task requirement.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Alternative song advice for fixed material; \'93perfect song choice\'94; invented song title/cut/time-limit/era rule.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R16\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F05, F14, F15\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S001\'96S020, S035\'96S040\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves material_policy guardrails.\cell \row

\clvertalc \clshdrawnil \clwWidth1566\clftsWidth3 \clmart10 \clmarl10 \clmarb10 \clmarr10 \clbrdrt\brdrnil \clbrdrl\brdrnil \clbrdrb\brdrnil \clbrdrr\brdrnil \clpadt20 \clpadl20 \clpadb20 \clpadr20 \gaph\cellx8640
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S12 \'97 Tape-observable versus live-room / process-only boundary\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Finished song tape can support observable singing, interpretation and assessability. It cannot prove response to direction, learning speed, aural skills, sight-singing, stamina, callback readiness, training potential or broad professional readiness.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Direct tape evidence or explicit process evidence if actually supplied.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S13 \'97 Vocal-health safety and no-diagnosis language\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Do not diagnose from tape. Strain-like, fatigued, hoarse, pressed or reduced-range sound may be described only as cautious observable sound, with assessability or care-seeking phrased non-diagnostically.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Clear audible evidence and evidence_sufficiency permitting vocal observation.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R12\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F11\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S043\'96S046\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S14 \'97 Accessibility, accent, speech difference and inclusive voice handling\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Access needs, disability, neurodivergence, hearing difference, speech difference, accent, gender-diverse voice, assistive technology and reasonable adjustments are access/fairness context, not deficits. Diction feedback must focus on intelligibility, not accent hierarchy.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Observable communication/audibility evidence plus supplied access context where available.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Accent prestige, gendered voice norms, body/appearance assumptions, access-support penalty, assistive-tech deficit language.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S15 \'97 Generic-feedback suppression and evidence anchors\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Every substantive strength, improvement, category note and fix-first item must attach to a behaviour, phrase, lyric, timestamp, section, style demand or assessability fact.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Observable evidence anchor, ideally timestamped or material-linked.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Generic vocal praise, generic story praise, unsupported style labels and \'93technically excellent vocal performance\'94 without evidence.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R03, R17, R21\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-S021\'96S031, 0D, report-polish controls\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 VOICE-REV-S16 \'97 Timestamped notes, fix-first and next-take specificity\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Timestamped notes should be chronological, specific and distributed across meaningful song moments where evidence exists. Fix-first must name the highest-impact observable change. Next-take advice must be component-aware and evidence-led.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Existing locked timestamps, phrase/lyric/technical moment, category link and practical next action.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Invented timestamps; generic drills; next-take advice unrelated to evidence; exceeding max 8 timestamps.\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 R18, R21\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 F17\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 0D, report-quality / report-polish controls\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Preserves max timestamp and locked evidence.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Voice / Singing revisions must not hide Vocal in genuine Song/MT, must not label Acting speech as singing, must not label Dance movement as singing/voice, and must not weaken MT acting + song flow.\cell

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 18. Revised Baseline Handoff for VOICE-FINAL-AUDIT\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-REV is complete with explicit limitations. The revised Voice / Singing baseline strengthens the current Song slice while preserving the live TapeCoach production architecture. It keeps the six operational score fields, Song and MT weights, server-side recomputation, caps, blockers, verdict logic, role-fit bounds, Step 1 / Step 2 architecture, locked evidence, deterministic scrubs, material-policy guardrails and MT acting + song anchor unchanged.\
The core revision clarifies that 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  means sung-vocal evidence in Song / Musical Theatre, while Acting/Monologue and Dance retain their existing proxy meanings and must not receive singing labels. Song remains vocal-centred, but acting/storytelling is now explicitly framed as lyric/storytelling and communication through song, not acting-scene performance. The revised evidence standard requires both vocal technique and interpretive evidence where assessable. Formal descriptor evidence is converted into observable prompt/report guidance for pitch, rhythm, tone, diction, phrasing, communication and interpretation, while external grades, marks and framework weights remain excluded.\
The revision adds song-framed acting-through-song, musical interpretation through existing fields, cautious style/subtype handling, context-specific accompaniment handling, and stronger separation of audio/technical assessability from vocal merit. It blocks production polish, expensive resources, paid accompanist access and studio quality as talent evidence. It preserves fixed-material policy and blocks false specificity such as invented song titles, universal cut lengths, universal era rules, unsupported voice-type fit and alternative material suggestions where material is fixed.\
Safety and fairness revisions add a firm no-diagnosis rule for vocal-health language and non-deficit handling of access needs, disability, neurodivergence, hearing difference, speech difference, accent, gender-diverse voice, reasonable adjustments and assistive technology. Generic vocal praise is now blocked unless anchored to observable evidence. Timestamp and next-take guidance is strengthened within existing caps and locked-evidence limits.\
Compact VOICE-REV-C changes: C01 architecture preservation; C02 sung-vocal semantics; C03 vocal-plus-interpretive standard; C04 descriptor specificity; C05 song-framed acting-through-song; C06 style/subtype limits; C07 assessability/anti-polish; C08 accompaniment context; C09 process-only boundary; C10 no-diagnosis safety; C11 inclusive voice guardrails; C12 material boundaries; C13 generic praise suppression; C14 timestamp/next-take specificity; C15 display deferral; C16 cross-discipline non-regression; C17 Step 2 locked-evidence preservation.\
Revised sections: VOICE-REV-S01\'96S18. Implemented recommendations: R01\'96R06, R08\'96R18. R07 is suppress conditionally / defer. R19 is deferred to output-specificity. R20\'96R21 are preserve-only / guardrail recommendations. Source range used: VOICE-S001\'96VOICE-S052. VOICE-SYN findings used: VOICE-SYN-F01\'96F17. Audit IDs used: VOICE-AUDIT-I01\'96I20 and VOICE-AUDIT-R01\'96R21.\
What was revised: baseline wording, evidence standards, claim-scope rules, style/subtype boundaries, safety/fairness guardrails, generic-feedback suppression and non-regression protections. What was not revised: scoring fields, weights, schema, backend, frontend, pipeline, caps, verdicts, role-fit logic, server-side recomputation or live-output behaviour.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 19. VOICE-REV Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 VOICE-REV complete with explicit limitations; ready for VOICE-FINAL-AUDIT.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale: the supplied VOICE-SYN and VOICE-AUDIT materials are sufficient to revise the Voice / Singing baseline at rubric/prompt/report-behaviour level without altering production architecture. The revision implements the evidence-backed, baseline-compatible recommendations and preserves all product constraints.\
Critical blockers: none for final audit of the revised baseline.\
Implemented recommendations: VOICE-AUDIT-R01\'96R06 and R08\'96R18.\
Preserve-only recommendations: VOICE-AUDIT-R20 and VOICE-AUDIT-R21.\
Suppressed conditionally / deferred recommendations: VOICE-AUDIT-R07 is limited to cautious evidence-led use; VOICE-AUDIT-R19 is deferred to output-specificity / display verification.\
Limitations to carry forward: thin belt/mix/registration evidence, uneven jazz/folk/commercial-pop descriptors, partial actor-musician evidence, no live Song / Voice outputs, unverified frontend labels, unverified comparison-page parity, unverified rendered timestamp parity and untested score stability.\
Live-output examples are 
\f0\b not required before VOICE-FINAL-AUDIT
\f1\b0  of the revised baseline, but they remain required later for output-specificity / live-output QA.\
Recommended mode for VOICE-FINAL-AUDIT: 
\f0\b standard ChatGPT with file uploads enabled, not Deep Research
\f1\b0 .\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
